查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem leNat_trans (a b c : Nat) (h1 : LeNat a b) (h2 : LeNat b c) : LeNat a c :=
  @LeNat.rec (fun (m n : Nat) (_ : LeNat m n) => LeNat a m → LeNat a n)
    (fun (n : Nat) => fun (h : LeNat a n) => h)
    (fun (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) (ih : LeNat a m → LeNat a n) =>
       fun (h1 : LeNat a m) => le_succ a n (ih h1))
    b c h2 h1
练习 2leNat_succ_succ定理附录解答

提示 1思路

对 h 用 LeNat.rec。动机是 LeNat (succ m) (succ n)；基例是 le_refl (succ n)，步例把 ih 用一次 le_succ 抬到 succ (succ n)。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat (Nat.succ a) (Nat.succ b)。

@LeNat.rec · le_refl · le_succ。

theorem leNat_succ_succ (a b : Nat) (h : LeNat a b) :
    LeNat (Nat.succ a) (Nat.succ b) :=
  @LeNat.rec (fun (m n : Nat) (_ : LeNat m n) => LeNat (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n))
    (fun (n : Nat) => le_refl (Nat.succ n))
    (fun (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) (ih : LeNat (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n)) =>
       le_succ (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) ih)
    a b h

对 k 用 Nat.rec（这里只有一个参数，直接归纳）。基例 k = 0 就是 h —— a + 0 定义性等于 a；步例要用 leNat_succ_succ，因为 a + succ k 定义性等于 succ (a + k)。

    h
    (fun (k : Nat) (ih : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) =>
       leNat_succ_succ (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k) ih)
    k

⚠ 消除方式：LeNat 有两个指标（m 与 n）。本语言的 match 只把 参数代进动机，指标仍留在分支里，所以对 h : LeNat m n 不能写

II.1 · 本章第 14 / 19 单元 · 全书第 14 / 171 单元

14ℕ 序的加法消去律

Unit 14 — Cancelling an Addend in the Order

4 个定义 · 14 个定理 · 1 个归纳类型 · 5 道练习 · 15 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑬ 证了「同加一个数保持序」（leNat_add_right）。要把序良定义地搬到 ℤ 上，还需要反方向的消去律：

m+k≤n+k⇒m≤n

直接对 k 归纳走不通：归纳到后继会卡在「succ m ≤ succ n ⇒ m ≤ n」——LeNat 的证明没法直接拆掉一层。所以改走两步：① 把序刻画成「存在一个加数」：LeNat m n ⇔ ∃ k, n = m + k；② 消去律落到等式层：a + k = b + k ⇒ a = b（对 k 归纳，用 Nat.succ.inj）。下一单元用这条消去律证整数序的良定义性（leRep_wd）。

本单元的 5 道练习就是这条论证链：等式消去 → 序的刻画 → 刻画的使用 → 序的消去。

归纳类型LeNat声明 1
inductive LeNat : Nat → Nat → Prop
ctor le_refl (n : Nat) : LeNat n n
ctor le_succ (m : Nat) (n : Nat) : LeNat m n → LeNat m (Nat.succ n)
end
定义intRel声明 2
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 3
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义leRep声明 4
def leRep (p q : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  LeNat (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
        (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))

以下四条是单元③的 ℕ 加法律，本单元自用（L3：律必须留在单元里）。

定理zero_add声明 5
theorem zero_add (b : Nat) :
    Nat.add Nat.zero b = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add Nat.zero n = n)
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add Nat.zero n = n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add Nat.zero n) n ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n))))
    b
定理succ_add声明 6
theorem succ_add (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.succ (Nat.add a b) :=

    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) ih

定理add_assoc声明 7
theorem add_assoc (a b c : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) c = Nat.add a (Nat.add b c) :=

    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a b))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n = Nat.add a (Nat.add b n)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat

定理add_comm声明 8
theorem add_comm (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add a b = Nat.add b a :=
证明 · 1 步 · 10 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero a) a (zero_add a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ m)
          (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add n a) ih
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n))))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (succ_add n a)))
    b

前驱：pred (succ n) = n 是定义性相等；用于从前驱等式换回原等式。

定义Nat.pred声明 9
def Nat.pred (n : Nat) : Nat := match n with
| zero => Nat.zero
| succ m => m

本语言没有内建的 Nat.succ.inj：对等式两边取前驱即得（定义性相等）。

定理Nat.succ.inj声明 10
theorem Nat.succ.inj (a b : Nat) (h : Nat.succ a = Nat.succ b) :
    a = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   congrArg.{1} Nat.pred h

转动加数：把 (a+k)+j 换成 (a+j)+k（消去律最后一步要用）。

定理add_rotate声明 11
theorem add_rotate (a k j : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j = Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k :=
证明 · 1 步 · 5 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
    (add_assoc a k j)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) (add_comm k j))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (add_assoc a j k)))

练习 1nat_add_cancel_right定理附录解答
theorem nat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat) :
    Nat.add a k = Nat.add b k → a = b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 k 归纳。基例 k = 0 时 a + 0 定义性等于 a，假设 h 的类型就是 a = b；步例 k = succ n 时两边各露一层 succ，用 Nat.succ.inj 剥掉后再交给归纳假设。

提示 2目标形态

a + k = b + k → a = b（对 k 归纳，动机把两边都带上）。

提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Nat.succ.inj（本文件已给）。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行
theorem nat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat) :
    Nat.add a k = Nat.add b k → a = b :=
  Nat.rec
    (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b)
    (fun (h : a = b) => h)
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b) =>
      fun (h : Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ (Nat.add b n)) =>
        ih (Nat.succ.inj (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add b n) h))
    k
练习 2leNat_exists_add定理附录解答
theorem leNat_exists_add (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) :
    ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 h 用 LeNat.rec（两个指标 + 证明都要进 motive）。基例 le_refl n：取加数 0，n + 0 定义性等于 n；步例 le_succ：把归纳假设给的存在加数 k 抬成 succ k —— m + succ k 定义性等于 succ (m + k)，再用 congrArg 把等式包一层 succ。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat m n → ∃ (k : Nat), n = m + k。

提示 3关键件

@LeNat.rec · @Exists.intro（隐式位要写全：类型、谓词）· @Exists.elim · congrArg。

查看解答 定理 · 12 行
theorem leNat_exists_add (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) :
    ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k :=
  @LeNat.rec
    (fun (m n : Nat) (_ : LeNat m n) =>
      ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k)
    (fun (n : Nat) =>
      Exists.intro Nat.zero (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat n))
    (fun (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n)
         (ih : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) =>
      Exists.elim ih (fun (k : Nat) (hk : n = Nat.add m k) =>
          Exists.intro (Nat.succ k) (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ hk)))
    m n h
练习 3leNat_self_add定理附录解答
theorem leNat_self_add (m k : Nat) : LeNat m (Nat.add m k) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 k 归纳。基例是 le_refl m（m + 0 定义性等于 m）；步例把归纳假设 LeNat m (m + k) 用一次 le_succ 抬到 succ (m + k)，而 m + succ k 定义性就是它。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat m (m + k)。

提示 3关键件

Nat.rec（动机 fun n => LeNat m (m + n)）· le_refl · le_succ。

查看解答 定理 · 5 行
theorem leNat_self_add (m k : Nat) : LeNat m (Nat.add m k) :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => LeNat m (Nat.add m n))
    (le_refl m)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : LeNat m (Nat.add m n)) => le_succ m (Nat.add m n) ih)
    k
练习 4leNat_of_exists_add定理附录解答
theorem leNat_of_exists_add (m n : Nat)
    (h : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) :
    LeNat m n :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

从存在假设里取出加数 k 与等式 hk，用 leNat_self_add 得到 LeNat m (m + k)，再沿 m + k = n（hk 取对称）把目标换掉。

提示 2目标形态

(∃ (k : Nat), n = m + k) → LeNat m n。

提示 3关键件

@Exists.elim · leNat_self_add（练习 ③）· @Eq.subst（谓词 fun x => LeNat m x）· @Eq.symm。

查看解答 定理 · 7 行
theorem leNat_of_exists_add (m n : Nat)
    (h : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) :
    LeNat m n :=
  Exists.elim h (fun (k : Nat) (hk : n = Nat.add m k) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat m x) (Nat.add m k) n
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat n (Nat.add m k) hk)
        (leNat_self_add m k))
练习 5leNat_add_cancel_right定理附录解答
theorem leNat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) : LeNat a b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先用 leNat_exists_add（练习 ②）把 h 变成 ∃ j, b + k = (a + k) + j；取出的 j 就是见证。把右边用 add_rotate 转成 (a + j) + k，于是 b + k = (a + j) + k，交给 nat_add_cancel_right（练习 ①）得 b = a + j，最后用 leNat_of_exists_add（练习 ④）收口。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat (a + k) (b + k) → LeNat a b。

提示 3关键件

leNat_exists_add · add_rotate（本文件已给）· nat_add_cancel_right · leNat_of_exists_add · @Eq.trans。

查看解答 定理 · 10 行
theorem leNat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) : LeNat a b :=
  leNat_of_exists_add a b
    (Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k) h) (fun (j : Nat)
           (hj : Nat.add b k = Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) =>
        Exists.intro j (nat_add_cancel_right b (Nat.add a j) k
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add b k) (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j)
              (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
              hj
              (add_rotate a k j)))))

演示：2 + 3 ≤ 2 + 3 消去 3 得 2 ≤ 2。（要等练习 ⑤ 补完才会变成 checked。）

定理demo_leNat_cancel声明 19
theorem demo_leNat_cancel : LeNat 2 2 :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   leNat_add_cancel_right 2 2 3 (le_refl 5)

    Nat.add a k = Nat.add b k → a = b :=

    (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b)
    (fun (h : a = b) => h)
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b) =>
      fun (h : Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ (Nat.add b n)) =>
        ih (Nat.succ.inj (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add b n) h))
    k

练习 4 · leNat_of_exists_add 定理
theorem leNat_of_exists_add (m n : Nat)
    (h : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) :
    LeNat m n :=
  Exists.elim h (fun (k : Nat) (hk : n = Nat.add m k) =>

        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat n (Nat.add m k) hk)
        (leNat_self_add m k))
练习 5 · leNat_add_cancel_right 定理
theorem leNat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) : LeNat a b :=
  leNat_of_exists_add a b
    (Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k) h) (fun (j : Nat)
           (hj : Nat.add b k = Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) =>
        Exists.intro j (nat_add_cancel_right b (Nat.add a j) k
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add b k) (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j)
              (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
              hj
              (add_rotate a k j)))))

II.1 · 本章第 15 / 19 单元 · 全书第 15 / 171 单元

15整数序的良定义性

Unit 15 — Order is Well-Defined

4 个定义 · 23 个定理 · 1 个归纳类型 · 5 道练习 · 15 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑫ 把整数序写成了代表元公式：a - b ≤ c - d ⇔ a + d ≤ c + b。要把这个序搬到商类型 Int 上（Quot.lift / Quot.liftOn），必须先证它 不依赖代表元的选择：若 (a,b) ~ (a',b') 且 (c,d) ~ (c',d')，则 leRep (a,b) (c,d) ⇔ leRep (a',b') (c',d')。

两条假设按定义是 Hp : a + b' = b + a' （两个整数 a - b 与 a' - b' 相等）

Hq:c+d'=d+c'

证明的套路是「把差补平再消掉」：两边同加 b'（或 d'），再用 Hp / Hq 把新加的数搬到对面，最后对加法消去律用一次 leNat_add_cancel_right（单元⑭）。

归纳类型LeNat声明 1
inductive LeNat : Nat → Nat → Prop
ctor le_refl (n : Nat) : LeNat n n
ctor le_succ (m : Nat) (n : Nat) : LeNat m n → LeNat m (Nat.succ n)
end
定义intRel声明 2
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 3
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义leRep声明 4
def leRep (p q : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  LeNat (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
        (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))

以下四条是单元③的 ℕ 加法律，本单元自用（L3：律必须留在单元里）。

定理zero_add声明 5
theorem zero_add (b : Nat) :
    Nat.add Nat.zero b = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add Nat.zero n = n)
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add Nat.zero n = n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add Nat.zero n) n ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n))))
    b
定理succ_add声明 6
theorem succ_add (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.succ (Nat.add a b) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.succ (Nat.add a n))
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) n))))
    b
定理add_assoc声明 7
theorem add_assoc (a b c : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) c = Nat.add a (Nat.add b c) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n = Nat.add a (Nat.add b n))
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a b))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n = Nat.add a (Nat.add b n)) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n) (Nat.add a (Nat.add b n)) ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) n))))
    c
定理add_comm声明 8
theorem add_comm (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add a b = Nat.add b a :=
证明 · 1 步 · 10 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero a) a (zero_add a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ m)
          (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add n a) ih
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n))))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (succ_add n a)))
    b

前驱：pred (succ n) = n 是定义性相等；用于从前驱等式换回原等式。

定义Nat.pred声明 9
def Nat.pred (n : Nat) : Nat := match n with
| zero => Nat.zero
| succ m => m

本语言没有内建的 Nat.succ.inj：对等式两边取前驱即得（定义性相等）。

定理Nat.succ.inj声明 10
theorem Nat.succ.inj (a b : Nat) (h : Nat.succ a = Nat.succ b) :
    a = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 1 行
  1.   congrArg.{1} Nat.pred h

转动加数：把 (a+k)+j 换成 (a+j)+k（消去律最后一步要用）。

定理add_rotate声明 11
theorem add_rotate (a k j : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j = Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k :=
证明 · 1 步 · 5 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
    (add_assoc a k j)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) (add_comm k j))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (add_assoc a j k)))

演示：前驱把 succ 的等式还原。

定理nat_add_cancel_right声明 14
theorem nat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat) :
    Nat.add a k = Nat.add b k → a = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   Nat.rec
    (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b)
    (fun (h : a = b) => h)
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add b n → a = b) =>
      fun (h : Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ (Nat.add b n)) =>
        ih (Nat.succ.inj (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add b n) h))
    k
定理leNat_exists_add声明 15
theorem leNat_exists_add (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) :
    ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k :=
证明 · 1 步 · 10 行
  1.   @LeNat.rec
    (fun (m n : Nat) (_ : LeNat m n) =>
      ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k)
    (fun (n : Nat) =>
      Exists.intro Nat.zero (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat n))
    (fun (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n)
         (ih : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) =>
      Exists.elim ih (fun (k : Nat) (hk : n = Nat.add m k) =>
          Exists.intro (Nat.succ k) (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ hk)))
    m n h
定理leNat_self_add声明 16
theorem leNat_self_add (m k : Nat) : LeNat m (Nat.add m k) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => LeNat m (Nat.add m n))
    (le_refl m)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : LeNat m (Nat.add m n)) => le_succ m (Nat.add m n) ih)
    k
定理leNat_of_exists_add声明 17
theorem leNat_of_exists_add (m n : Nat)
    (h : ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k) :
    LeNat m n :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   Exists.elim h (fun (k : Nat) (hk : n = Nat.add m k) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat m x) (Nat.add m k) n
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat n (Nat.add m k) hk)
        (leNat_self_add m k))
定理leNat_add_cancel_right声明 18
theorem leNat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) : LeNat a b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行
  1.   leNat_of_exists_add a b
    (Exists.elim (leNat_exists_add (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k) h) (fun (j : Nat)
           (hj : Nat.add b k = Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) =>
        Exists.intro j (nat_add_cancel_right b (Nat.add a j) k
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add b k) (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j)
              (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
              hj
              (add_rotate a k j)))))

    (fun (n : Nat) => le_refl (Nat.succ n))
    (fun (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) (ih : LeNat (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n)) =>
       le_succ (Nat.succ m) (Nat.succ n) ih)
    a b h

把 intRel 假设直接读成等式：intRel (a,b) (x,y) 展开是 a + y = x + b。（假设位不会自动展开这个定义，先 Eq.subst 一次把等式拿到手。）

定理intRel_to_eq声明 23
theorem intRel_to_eq (a b x y : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat x y)) :
    Nat.add a y = Nat.add x b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a y = m)
    (Nat.add a y) (Nat.add x b) h
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a y))
练习 1intRel_symm定理附录解答
theorem intRel_symm (a b a1 b1 : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1)) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)  :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

intRel (a1,b1) (a,b) 展开是 a1 + b = a + b1，正是 intRel (a,b) (a1,b1)（a + b1 = a1 + b）取对称。

提示 2目标形态

a + b1 = a1 + b → a1 + b = a + b1。

提示 3关键件

intRel_to_eq（本文件已给）· Eq.symm。

查看解答 定理 · 4 行
theorem intRel_symm (a b a1 b1 : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1)) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  @Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add a b1) (Nat.add a1 b) (intRel_to_eq a b a1 b1 h)
练习 2leRep_wd_fst定理附录解答
theorem leRep_wd_fst (a b a1 b1 c d : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b1 = Nat.add a1 b)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)  :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把 h 用 intRel_to_eq 读成 Hp : a + b1 = a1 + b。两边同加 b1（leNat_add_right），左边 (a+d)+b1 用 add_rotate 转成 (a+b1)+d、用 Hp 换成 (a1+b)+d、再 rotate 成 (a1+d)+b；右边 rotate 成 (c+b1)+b。最后用 leNat_add_cancel_right 消去 b。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat (a+d) (c+b) → LeNat (a1+d) (c+b1)（Hp : a + b1 = a1 + b）。

提示 3关键件

intRel_to_eq · leNat_add_right · add_rotate · congrArg · Eq.trans · Eq.subst · leNat_add_cancel_right。

查看解答 定理 · 26 行
theorem leRep_wd_fst (a b a1 b1 c d : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b1 = Nat.add a1 b)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) :=
  @leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a1 d) (Nat.add c b1) b
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c b1) b))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 d) b)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 b) d)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 d) b)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b1) d)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 b) d)
          (add_rotate a d b1)
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x d) h))
        (add_rotate a1 b d))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) b1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b1) b)
        (add_rotate c b b1)
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) b1 hl)))
练习 3leRep_wd_snd定理附录解答
theorem leRep_wd_snd (a b c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d1 = Nat.add c1 d)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)  :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

结构对偶：先把 h 用 intRel_to_eq 读成 Hq : c + d1 = c1 + d。两边同加 d1，左边 rotate 成 (a+d1)+d；右边 (c+b)+d1 rotate 成 (c+d1)+b、用 Hq 换成 (c1+d)+b、再 rotate 成 (c1+b)+d。最后用 leNat_add_cancel_right 消去 d。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat (a+d) (c+b) → LeNat (a+d1) (c1+b)（Hq : c + d1 = c1 + d）。

提示 3关键件

intRel_to_eq · leNat_add_right · add_rotate · congrArg · Eq.trans · leNat_add_cancel_right。

查看解答 定理 · 26 行
theorem leRep_wd_snd (a b c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d1 = Nat.add c1 d)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) :=
  @leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a d1) (Nat.add c1 b) d
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) d1)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d1) d)
      (add_rotate a d d1)
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) d1) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 d) b)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c d1) b)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 d) b)
            (add_rotate c b d1)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x b) h))
          (add_rotate c1 d b))
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) d1 hl)))
练习 4leRep_wd定理附录解答
theorem leRep_wd (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)  :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先对第一对代表元用 leRep_wd_fst 得 leRep (a1,b1) (c,d)，再对第二对用 leRep_wd_snd。两条假设用 intRel_to_eq 各读成等式一次。

提示 2目标形态

leRep (a,b) (c,d) → leRep (a1,b1) (c1,d1)。

提示 3关键件

intRel_to_eq · leRep_wd_fst（练习 ②）· leRep_wd_snd（练习 ③）。

查看解答 定理 · 8 行
theorem leRep_wd (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) :=
  fun (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) =>
    leRep_wd_snd a1 b1 c d c1 d1 (intRel_to_eq c d c1 d1 hqq)
      (leRep_wd_fst a b a1 b1 c d (intRel_to_eq a b a1 b1 hpp) hl)
练习 5leRep_wd_iff定理附录解答
theorem leRep_wd_iff (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) ∧
        (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))  :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两个方向：正向就是练习 ④；反向把两条假设各取一次 intRel_symm（练习 ①），再对调两组代表元用一次练习 ④。用 And.intro 把两个方向装起来。

提示 2目标形态

(leRep p q → leRep p1 q1) ∧ (leRep p1 q1 → leRep p q)。

提示 3关键件

And.intro · leRep_wd（练习 ④）· intRel_symm（练习 ①）。

查看解答 定理 · 12 行
theorem leRep_wd_iff (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) ∧
        (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :=
  And.intro
    (leRep_wd a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 hpp hqq)
    (leRep_wd a1 b1 a b c1 d1 c d
      (intRel_symm a b a1 b1 hpp)
      (intRel_symm c d c1 d1 hqq))
编写注记 · 教材对照与课程边界（1 条）

本文件按 L3 自带所需的前置：单元③的 ℕ 加法律、单元⑬的序单调性、单元⑭的序消去律。本单元的 5 道练习是良定义性的分解：① 关系对称 → ② 第一个坐标不变 → ③ 第二个坐标不变 → ④ 两个坐标一起 → ⑤ 当且仅当。

整数序的良定义性

5 道练习 · 5 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · intRel_symm 定理
theorem intRel_symm (a b a1 b1 : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1)) :
    intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  @Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add a b1) (Nat.add a1 b) (intRel_to_eq a b a1 b1 h)
练习 2 · leRep_wd_fst 定理
theorem leRep_wd_fst (a b a1 b1 c d : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b1 = Nat.add a1 b)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) :=
  @leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a1 d) (Nat.add c b1) b
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c b1) b))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 d) b)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 b) d)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 d) b)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b1) d)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a1 b) d)
          (add_rotate a d b1)
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x d) h))
        (add_rotate a1 b d))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) b1) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) b1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b1) b)
        (add_rotate c b b1)
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) b1 hl)))
练习 3 · leRep_wd_snd 定理
theorem leRep_wd_snd (a b c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add c d1 = Nat.add c1 d)
    (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) :=
  @leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a d1) (Nat.add c1 b) d
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) d1)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d1) d)
      (add_rotate a d d1)
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) d1) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 d) b)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 b) d)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d1)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c d1) b)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add c1 d) b)
            (add_rotate c b d1)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x b) h))
          (add_rotate c1 d b))
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) d1 hl)))
练习 4 · leRep_wd 定理
theorem leRep_wd (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) :=
  fun (hl : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) =>
    leRep_wd_snd a1 b1 c d c1 d1 (intRel_to_eq c d c1 d1 hqq)
      (leRep_wd_fst a b a1 b1 c d (intRel_to_eq a b a1 b1 hpp) hl)
练习 5 · leRep_wd_iff 定理
theorem leRep_wd_iff (a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 : Nat)
    (hpp : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1))
    (hqq : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) :
    (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1)) ∧
        (leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a1 b1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c1 d1) →
         leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :=
  And.intro
    (leRep_wd a b a1 b1 c d c1 d1 hpp hqq)
    (leRep_wd a1 b1 a b c1 d1 c d
      (intRel_symm a b a1 b1 hpp)
      (intRel_symm c d c1 d1 hqq))

II.1 · 本章第 16 / 19 单元 · 全书第 16 / 171 单元

16整数序的传递性与加法单调

Unit 16 — Transitivity and Translation Invariance

5 个定义 · 19 个定理 · 1 个归纳类型 · 2 道练习 · 7 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑮ 把整数序 leRep 证成了良定义的关系：换代表元不改变真假。按单元⑦的教训（本语言没有 funext，也还没有把关系「下降」成 Int → Int → Prop 的机制），序公理继续在代表元层陈述：

a−b≤c−d⇔a+d≤c+b

它们是整数序的三条核心性质中的两条（自反已在单元⑫）：① 传递：a ≤ b 且 b ≤ c ⇒ a ≤ c；② 平移不变：a ≤ b ⇒ a + r ≤ b + r（本单元做右加；左加留给读者用交换律推出）。证明只用到单元⑬的 ℕ 序传递 / 右加单调、单元⑭的序消去律，以及一个纯算术的四项交换引理 add4（本文件已给）。

归纳类型LeNat声明 1
inductive LeNat : Nat → Nat → Prop
ctor le_refl (n : Nat) : LeNat n n
ctor le_succ (m : Nat) (n : Nat) : LeNat m n → LeNat m (Nat.succ n)
end
定义intRel声明 2
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 3
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义leRep声明 4
def leRep (p q : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  LeNat (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))
        (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat q) (Prod.snd Nat Nat p))
定义addRep声明 5
def addRep (p q : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat p) (Prod.fst Nat Nat q))
    (Nat.add (Prod.snd Nat Nat p) (Prod.snd Nat Nat q))

以下四条是单元③的 ℕ 加法律，本单元自用（L3：律必须留在单元里）。

定理zero_add声明 6
theorem zero_add (b : Nat) :
    Nat.add Nat.zero b = b :=

  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add Nat.zero n = n)
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add Nat.zero n = n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add Nat.zero n) n ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n))))
    b

定理add_comm声明 9
theorem add_comm (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add a b = Nat.add b a :=
证明 · 1 步 · 10 行
  1.   Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a)
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero a) a (zero_add a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a) =>

          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ m)

        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (succ_add n a)))
    b

前驱：pred (succ n) = n 是定义性相等；用于从前驱等式换回原等式。

定义Nat.pred声明 10
def Nat.pred (n : Nat) : Nat := match n with
| zero => Nat.zero
| succ m => m

本语言没有内建的 Nat.succ.inj：对等式两边取前驱即得（定义性相等）。

    a = b :=

转动加数：把 (a+k)+j 换成 (a+j)+k（消去律最后一步要用）。

定理add_rotate声明 12
theorem add_rotate (a k j : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j = Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k :=
证明 · 1 步 · 5 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j) (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
    (add_assoc a k j)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add k j)) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
      (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) (add_comm k j))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k) (Nat.add a (Nat.add j k)) (add_assoc a j k)))

ℕ 序的传递性（单元⑬）：对 h2 归纳，动机取 LeNat a m → LeNat a n。

       fun (h1 : LeNat a m) => le_succ a n (ih h1))
    b c h2 h1

定理leNat_exists_add声明 15
theorem leNat_exists_add (m n : Nat) (h : LeNat m n) :
    ∃ (k : Nat), n = Nat.add m k :=

theorem leNat_add_cancel_right (a b k : Nat)
    (h : LeNat (Nat.add a k) (Nat.add b k)) : LeNat a b :=

        Exists.intro j (nat_add_cancel_right b (Nat.add a j) k
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add b k) (Nat.add (Nat.add a k) j)
              (Nat.add (Nat.add a j) k)
              hj
              (add_rotate a k j)))))

四项交换：(a+d)+(e+f) = (a+e)+(d+f)。纯 ℕ 算术（结合 + 交换），两条平移不变性都要用它把「先加在哪一侧」对齐。

定理add4声明 21
theorem add4 (a d e f : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add e f) = Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 28 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add e f))
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add e (Nat.add d f)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add e f))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add d (Nat.add e f)))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add e (Nat.add d f)))
      (add_assoc a d (Nat.add e f))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add d (Nat.add e f)))
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add d e) f))
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add e (Nat.add d f)))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x)
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add d e) f) (Nat.add d (Nat.add e f))
            (add_assoc d e f)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add d e) f))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add e d) f))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add e (Nat.add d f)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x f) (add_comm d e)))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x)
            (add_assoc e d f)))))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add e (Nat.add d f)))
      (add_assoc a e (Nat.add d f)))
练习 1leRep_trans定理附录解答
theorem leRep_trans (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h1 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (h2 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

两条假设在代表元层是 H1 : a+d ≤ c+b、H2 : c+f ≤ e+d。目标是 a+f ≤ e+b。

提示 2思路

H1 两边同加 f 得 (a+d)+f ≤ (c+b)+f，用 add_rotate 两边各转一次变成 (a+f)+d ≤ (c+f)+b；H2 两边同加 b 得 (c+f)+b ≤ (e+d)+b，再把右边转成 (e+b)+d。传递后对 d 用 leNat_add_cancel_right。

提示 3目标形态

leRep (a,b) (e,f) 即 LeNat (a+f) (e+b)。

提示 4关键件

leNat_add_right · add_rotate · Eq.subst · leNat_trans · leNat_add_cancel_right。

查看解答 定理 · 18 行
theorem leRep_trans (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h1 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (h2 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) :=
  leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a f) (Nat.add e b) d
    (leNat_trans (Nat.add (Nat.add a f) d) (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add e b) d)
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) f) (Nat.add (Nat.add a f) d) (add_rotate a d f)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) f) x)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) f) (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b) (add_rotate c b f)
          (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) f h1)))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add e d) b) (Nat.add (Nat.add e b) d) (add_rotate e d b)
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add c f) (Nat.add e d) b h2)))
练习 2leRep_add_right定理附录解答
theorem leRep_add_right (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
          (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

h : a+d ≤ c+b；两边同加 (e+f) 得 (a+d)+(e+f) ≤ (c+b)+(e+f)。用 add4 把左边改成 (a+e)+(d+f)、右边改成 (c+e)+(b+f)，正是 leRep (addRep (a,b) (e,f)) (addRep (c,d) (e,f)) 展开后的两层。

提示 2目标形态

LeNat ((a+e)+(d+f)) ((c+e)+(b+f))。

提示 3关键件

leNat_add_right · add4（本文件已给）· Eq.subst。

查看解答 定理 · 13 行
theorem leRep_add_right (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
          (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f)) x)
    (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f)) (Nat.add (Nat.add c e) (Nat.add b f))
    (add4 c b e f)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add e f)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f))
      (add4 a d e f)
      (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f) h))

演示：具体数字上传递一次 —— 0 ≤ 1 ≤ 2 推出 0 ≤ 2（都是 0 - 0 ≤ n - 0）。

    (le_succ Nat.zero Nat.zero (le_refl Nat.zero))
    (le_succ 1 1 (le_refl 1))

演示：(1-0) ≤ (3-0) 两边同加 (2-1) ⇒ (3-1) ≤ (5-1)。

单元⑯ 整数序的传递性与加法单调（序公理，代表元层）

整数序的传递性与加法单调

2 道练习 · 2 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · leRep_trans 定理
theorem leRep_trans (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h1 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (h2 : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :
    leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f) :=
  leNat_add_cancel_right (Nat.add a f) (Nat.add e b) d
    (leNat_trans (Nat.add (Nat.add a f) d) (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b)
      (Nat.add (Nat.add e b) d)
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) f) (Nat.add (Nat.add a f) d) (add_rotate a d f)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) f) x)
          (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) f) (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b) (add_rotate c b f)
          (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) f h1)))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add c f) b) x)
        (Nat.add (Nat.add e d) b) (Nat.add (Nat.add e b) d) (add_rotate e d b)
        (leNat_add_right (Nat.add c f) (Nat.add e d) b h2)))
练习 2 · leRep_add_right 定理
theorem leRep_add_right (a b c d e f : Nat)
    (h : leRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :
    leRep (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f))
          (addRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat e f)) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f)) x)
    (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f)) (Nat.add (Nat.add c e) (Nat.add b f))
    (add4 c b e f)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
      (fun (x : Nat) => LeNat x (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add e f)) (Nat.add (Nat.add a e) (Nat.add d f))
      (add4 a d e f)
      (leNat_add_right (Nat.add a d) (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add e f) h))

II.1 · 本章第 17 / 19 单元 · 全书第 17 / 171 单元

17整数乘法的交换律（代表元层）

Unit 17 — Multiplication is Commutative

5 个定义 · 14 个定理 · 3 道练习 · 9 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑪ 证完了「第一个因子换代表元不改变乘积」。要把乘法真正放到 Int 上，还差两件：第二个因子换代表元也不改变乘积（这要用交换律），以及乘法自身的交换 / 结合 / 分配律。本单元先做第一件的一半：乘法的交换律。

路线（全部先落在 ℕ 上，再逐分量搬到代表元层）：

zero_mul:0×a=0

succ_mul : (a+1) * b = a*b + b （对 b 归纳）myMul_comm : a * b = b * a （用 succ_mul 换掉左边多出来的那个 a）mulRep_comm : (a,b) * (c,d) = (c,d) * (a,b)（按定义逐分量，用 myMul_comm）

为什么先做这个：mulRep_wd 固定的是第二个分量；要得到「第二个因子换代表元也不改变乘积」，必须先把乘法的两个分量对调（mulRep_comm + intRel 的对称性）。没有它，Int 上的乘法连「两个因子都良定义」都说不出口，更不用说 ℚ。

定义intRel声明 1
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 2
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义intOfNat声明 3
def intOfNat (n : Nat) : Int :=
  @Quot.mk.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat n Nat.zero)

递归步直接用 prelude 的 Nat.add（与单元⑤的 myAdd 逐点相等；本语言里 Nat.add 是原生自指定义，两者不是定义性相等）。

定义myMul声明 4
def myMul (a b : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.rec (fun (_ : Nat) => Nat) Nat.zero
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat) => Nat.add a ih) b
定义mulRep声明 5
def mulRep (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b)))

单元③的 ℕ 零元律，本单元自用（L3：律必须留在单元里）。

以下四条是单元③的 ℕ 加法律，本单元自用（L3：律必须留在单元里）。

theorem zero_add (b : Nat) :
    Nat.add Nat.zero b = b :=
证明 · 1 步 · 8 行

    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add Nat.zero n = n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n) = Nat.succ m)
        (Nat.add Nat.zero n) n ih
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add Nat.zero n))))
    b

theorem add_comm (a b : Nat) :
    Nat.add a b = Nat.add b a :=

    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add Nat.zero a) a (zero_add a))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat.add a n = Nat.add n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n)) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.succ (Nat.add a n) = Nat.succ m)
          (Nat.add a n) (Nat.add n a) ih
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add a n))))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.succ (Nat.add n a)) (succ_add n a)))
    b

定理add_shift声明 11
theorem add_shift (a b c d : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d) =
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 36 行
  1.   @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d))
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
    (add_assoc a c (Nat.add b d))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
      (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) = Nat.add a m)
        (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)) (add_assoc c b d))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))
        (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d) = Nat.add a (Nat.add m d))
          (Nat.add c b) (Nat.add b c)
          (add_comm c b)
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add c b) d))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))
          (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d) = Nat.add a m)
            (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d) (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d))
            (add_assoc b c d)
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (Nat.add (Nat.add b c) d))))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))
            (Nat.add a (Nat.add b (Nat.add c d)))
            (add_assoc a b (Nat.add c d))))))

练习 1succ_mul定理附录解答
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形两边都归约成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+1) + myMul (a+1) n，用 ih 把 myMul (a+1) n 换成 a*n + n，再用 succ_add / add_assoc 把两边对到 succ (a + (a*n + n))。

提示 2目标形态
(a+1)×b=a×b+b
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Eq.subst（把 ih 换进 add (a+1) 的第二个位置）· succ_add · add_assoc · Eq.refl（add x (succ n) 定义性归约）。

查看解答 定理 · 27 行
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
            (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n)
                (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)) (add_assoc a (myMul a n) n)))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))))))
    b
练习 2myMul_comm定理附录解答
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形用 zero_mul 把两边都变成 0；后继情形 myMul a (n+1) = a + myMul a n，用 ih 换成 a + myMul n a，再用 add_comm 换成 myMul n a + a，最后用 succ_mul 的对称收口。

提示 2目标形态
myMulab=myMulba
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · congrArg（在 add a 下改写 ih）· add_comm · succ_mul（取对称）。

查看解答 定理 · 15 行
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a n = myMul n a)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero a)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul Nat.zero a) Nat.zero (zero_mul a)))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add a (myMul n a)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) ih)
            (add_comm a (myMul n a)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (succ_mul n a))))
    b
练习 3mulRep_comm定理附录解答
theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

逐分量证明两个 prod_mk 相等。第一个分量用两次 congrArg（myMul_comm a c 与 myMul_comm b d）；第二个分量先把 a*d 与 b*c 对调，再用 add_comm 把两个加数顺序换过来；最后两次 congrArg 把分量相等装回序对。

提示 2目标形态
mulRep(a,b)(c,d)=mulRep(c,d)(a,b)
提示 3关键件

congrArg（作用在 fun x => prod_mk x … 与 fun y => prod_mk … y 上）· myMul_comm · add_comm · Eq.trans。

查看解答 定理 · 30 行
theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
      (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (x : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat x (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b d)) (myMul_comm a c))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul c a) y) (myMul_comm b d))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (y : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b)) y)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
        (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul c b))
        (Nat.add (myMul c b) (myMul d a))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
          (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul b c))
          (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul c b))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b c)) (myMul_comm a d))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul d a) y) (myMul_comm b c)))
        (add_comm (myMul d a) (myMul c b))))

编写注记 · 教材对照与课程边界（1 条）

本单元自带全部 ℕ 加法律（L3：律必须留在单元里；它们来自单元③）。

整数乘法的交换律（代表元层）

3 道练习 · 3 份按名匹配的解答

练习 1 · succ_mul 定理
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
            (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
            (congrArg.{1} Nat.succ
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n)
                (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)) (add_assoc a (myMul a n) n)))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.succ (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) n))))))
    b
练习 2 · myMul_comm 定理
theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul a n = myMul n a)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero (myMul Nat.zero a)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul Nat.zero a) Nat.zero (zero_mul a)))
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul a (Nat.succ n)) (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (myMul (Nat.succ n) a)
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add a (myMul n a)) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a)
            (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add a x) ih)
            (add_comm a (myMul n a)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ n) a) (Nat.add (myMul n a) a) (succ_mul n a))))
    b
练习 3 · mulRep_comm 定理
theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
      (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (x : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat x (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b d)) (myMul_comm a c))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul c a) y) (myMul_comm b d))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (y : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b)) y)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
        (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul c b))
        (Nat.add (myMul c b) (myMul d a))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
          (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul b c))
          (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul c b))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b c)) (myMul_comm a d))
          (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul d a) y) (myMul_comm b c)))
        (add_comm (myMul d a) (myMul c b))))

II.1 · 本章第 18 / 19 单元 · 全书第 18 / 171 单元

18整数乘法的第二个因子良定义（代表元层）

Unit 18 — Multiplication is Well-Defined in the Second Factor

5 个定义 · 21 个定理 · 8 道练习 · 24 条提示 · 解答附录

↑ 目录

单元⑪ 证了 mulRep_wd：第一个因子换代表元，乘积不变。单元⑰ 证了乘法的交换律 mulRep_comm。本单元把这两件事合起来，补上第二个因子换代表元乘积也不变：

intRel_symm:intRelpq⇒intRelqp
mulRep_wd_snd:p~p'⇒q×p~q×p'

为什么这是 Int 上乘法的最后一块：Quot.lift 要求「代表元换掉，值不变」。第一个因子的义务由 mulRep_wd 提供，第二个因子的义务由本单元的 mulRep_wd_snd 提供 —— 两条都齐，才能把 fun (p q : 代表元) => mk (mulRep p q) 下降成 Int → Int → Int。

定义intRel声明 1
def intRel (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prop :=
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b) =
  Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a)
定义Int声明 2
def Int : Type := @Quot.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel
定义intOfNat声明 3
def intOfNat (n : Nat) : Int :=
  @Quot.mk.{1} (Prod Nat Nat) intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat n Nat.zero)

递归步直接用 prelude 的 Nat.add（与单元⑤的 myAdd 逐点相等；本语言里 Nat.add 是原生自指定义，两者不是定义性相等）。

定义myMul声明 4
def myMul (a b : Nat) : Nat :=
  Nat.rec (fun (_ : Nat) => Nat) Nat.zero
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : Nat) => Nat.add a ih) b
定义mulRep声明 5
def mulRep (a b : Prod Nat Nat) : Prod Nat Nat :=
  @prod_mk Nat Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b)))
    (Nat.add (myMul (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
             (myMul (Prod.snd Nat Nat a) (Prod.fst Nat Nat b)))

theorem add_shift (a b c d : Nat) :
    Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d) =
    Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d) :=
证明 · 1 步 · 36 行

    (Nat.add (Nat.add a c) (Nat.add b d))
    (Nat.add a (Nat.add c (Nat.add b d)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add c d))

      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat

练习 1succ_mul定理附录解答
theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形两边都归约成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+1) + myMul (a+1) n，用 ih 把 myMul (a+1) n 换成 a*n + n，再用 succ_add / add_assoc 把两边对到 succ (a + (a*n + n))。

提示 2目标形态
(a+1)×b=a×b+b
提示 3关键件

Nat.rec · Eq.subst（把 ih 换进 add (a+1) 的第二个位置）· succ_add · add_assoc · Eq.refl（add x (succ n) 定义性归约）。

theorem succ_mul (a b : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.succ a) b = Nat.add (myMul a b) b :=
  Nat.rec (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n)
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
      (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (Nat.add (myMul a Nat.zero) Nat.zero) Nat.zero (add_zero (myMul a Nat.zero))))
    (fun (n : Nat)
         (ih : myMul (Nat.succ a) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) n) =>
      @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.succ a) m =
        Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) (myMul (Nat.succ a) n)
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.succ a) n) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n) ih)
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.succ a) (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))
          (Nat.succ (Nat.add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.succ n))
          (succ_add a (Nat.add (myMul a n) n))

theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :

提示 1思路

对 b 归纳。零情形用 zero_mul 把两边都变成 0；后继情形 myMul a (n+1) = a + myMul a n，用 ih 换成 a + myMul n a，再用 add_comm 换成 myMul n a + a，最后用 succ_mul 的对称收口。

theorem myMul_comm (a b : Nat) :
    myMul a b = myMul b a :=

    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul a n = myMul n a) =>

        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add a (myMul a n)))

练习 3mulRep_comm定理附录解答
theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

逐分量证明两个 prod_mk 相等。第一个分量用两次 congrArg（myMul_comm a c 与 myMul_comm b d）；第二个分量先把 a*d 与 b*c 对调，再用 add_comm 把两个加数顺序换过来；最后两次 congrArg 把分量相等装回序对。

mulRep(a,b)(c,d)=mulRep(c,d)(a,b)

theorem mulRep_comm (a b c d : Nat) :
    mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) =
      mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (@prod_mk Nat Nat
      (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
      (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (x : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat x (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul b d))
        (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (x : Nat) => Nat.add x (myMul b d)) (myMul_comm a c))
        (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul c a) y) (myMul_comm b d))))
    (congrArg.{1}
      (fun (y : Nat) => @prod_mk Nat Nat (Nat.add (myMul c a) (myMul d b)) y)
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
        (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul c b))
        (Nat.add (myMul c b) (myMul d a))

          (Nat.add (myMul d a) (myMul b c))

          (congrArg.{1} (fun (y : Nat) => Nat.add (myMul d a) y) (myMul_comm b c)))

练习 4mul_add定理附录解答

分配律（右）：(a + b) * c = a * c + b * c。

theorem mul_add (a b c : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.add a b) c = Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

对 c 归纳。零情形两边都算成 0；后继情形把左边展开成 (a+b) + myMul (a+b) n，用归纳假设换掉内层，再用 add_shift 把 (a+b)+(X+Y) 与 (a+X)+(b+Y) 对上。

提示 2目标形态

(a + b) * c = a * c + b * c（ℕ 上对第二个因子归纳）。

提示 3关键件

Nat.rec（动机 myMul (a+b) n = …）· Eq.subst（谓词 (a+b) + m）· add_shift（取对称）· Eq.trans。

查看解答 定理 · 21 行
theorem mul_add (a b c : Nat) :
    myMul (Nat.add a b) c = Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b c) :=
  Nat.rec
    (fun (n : Nat) => myMul (Nat.add a b) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) (myMul b n))
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat Nat.zero)
    (fun (n : Nat) (ih : myMul (Nat.add a b) n = Nat.add (myMul a n) (myMul b n)) =>
      @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add (myMul a n) (myMul b n)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add b (myMul b n)))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n) = Nat.add (Nat.add a b) m)
          (myMul (Nat.add a b) n)
          (Nat.add (myMul a n) (myMul b n))
          ih
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add a b) (Nat.succ n))))
        (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a (myMul a n)) (Nat.add b (myMul b n)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add a b) (Nat.add (myMul a n) (myMul b n)))
          (add_shift a b (myMul a n) (myMul b n))))
    c

等式两边同时乘一个数：把假设 h 沿 fun x => myMul x c 搬过去。

定理mul_congr声明 19
theorem mul_congr (a b a' b' c : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    myMul (Nat.add a b') c = myMul (Nat.add a' b) c :=
证明 · 1 步 · 4 行
  1.   @Eq.subst.{1} Nat
    (fun (x : Nat) => myMul (Nat.add a b') c = myMul x c)
    (Nat.add a b') (Nat.add a' b) h
    (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (myMul (Nat.add a b') c))
练习 5mul_shift_c定理附录解答

h 乘开：a·c + b'·c = a'·c + b·c。

theorem mul_shift_c (a b a' b' c : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c) =
    Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先把 a*c + b'*c 用 mul_add 的对称收回成 (a+b')*c，再用 h 把 (a+b') 换成 (a'+b)，最后用 mul_add 展开。

提示 2目标形态
a+b'=a'+b⇒a×c+b'×c=a'×c+b×c
提示 3关键件

mul_add（两次，一次取对称）· mul_congr · Eq.trans。

查看解答 定理 · 18 行
theorem mul_shift_c (a b a' b' c : Nat)
    (h : Nat.add a b' = Nat.add a' b) :
    Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c) =
    Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
    (myMul (Nat.add a b') c)
    (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
    (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add a b') c)
      (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
      (mul_add a b' c))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (myMul (Nat.add a b') c)
      (myMul (Nat.add a' b) c)
      (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
      (mul_congr a b a' b' c h)
      (mul_add a' b c))
练习 6mulRep_wd定理附录解答

良定义性（第二个分量固定）：p ~ p' ⇒ p * q ~ p' * q。

theorem mulRep_wd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

全部化到 ℕ 加法。左端 (ac+bd)+(a'd+b'c) 先重排成 (ac+b'c)+(bd+a'd)；用 mul_shift_c 换掉第一项；用 mul_shift_c（d）加交换换掉第二项；最后重排成 (a'c+b'd)+(ad+bc)。

提示 2目标形态

(ac+bd)+(a'd+b'c) = (a'c+b'd)+(ad+bc)（就是 intRel 展开）。

提示 3关键件

mul_shift_c（c 与 d 各一次）· add_shift · add_comm · Eq.subst · Eq.trans（每个中间项都写清）。

查看解答 定理 · 87 行
theorem mulRep_wd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)) :=
  @Eq.trans.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
             (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
             (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
    (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
             (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
               (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d))
               (Nat.add (myMul b' c) (myMul a' d)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
               (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c)) =
          Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) m)
        (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c))
        (Nat.add (myMul b' c) (myMul a' d))
        (add_comm (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c))
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b d)) (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b' c)))))
      (add_shift (myMul a c) (myMul b' c) (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
    (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
               (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
               (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
      (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
               (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
      (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
        (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)) =
          Nat.add m (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
        (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c))
        (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
        (mul_shift_c a b a' b' c h)
        (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a c) (myMul b' c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))))
      (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                 (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                 (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
        (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                 (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
        (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
          (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)) =
            Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) m)
          (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
          (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
          (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
            (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
            (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
            (add_comm (myMul b d) (myMul a' d))
            (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
              (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
              (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))
              (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d))
              (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
                (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))
                (Nat.add (myMul a' d) (myMul b d))
                (mul_shift_c a b a' b' d h))
              (add_comm (myMul a d) (myMul b' d))))
          (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c)) (Nat.add (myMul b d) (myMul a' d)))))
        (@Eq.trans.{1} Nat
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                   (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                   (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)))
          (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                   (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c)))
          (@Eq.symm.{1} Nat
            (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d))
                     (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)))
            (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b c))
                     (Nat.add (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
            (add_shift (myMul a' c) (myMul b c) (myMul b' d) (myMul a d)))
          (@Eq.subst.{1} Nat
            (fun (m : Nat) => Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d)) =
              Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) m)
            (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (Nat.add (myMul a d) (myMul b c))
            (add_comm (myMul b c) (myMul a d))
            (@Eq.refl.{1} Nat (Nat.add (Nat.add (myMul a' c) (myMul b' d)) (Nat.add (myMul b c) (myMul a d))))))))

⑱.1 intRel 的对称性

练习 7intRel_symm定理附录解答

intRel 说的是「交叉和相等」；把那条等式左右对调，就得到对称性。

theorem intRel_symm (a b : Prod Nat Nat)
    (h : intRel a b) : intRel b a :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

intRel 展开就是一条 ℕ 等式，取它的对称即可。

提示 2目标形态
intRelpq⇒intRelqp
提示 3关键件

Eq.symm（写全宇宙层级与论域）。

查看解答 定理 · 6 行
theorem intRel_symm (a b : Prod Nat Nat)
    (h : intRel a b) : intRel b a :=
  @Eq.symm.{1} Nat
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat a) (Prod.snd Nat Nat b))
    (Nat.add (Prod.fst Nat Nat b) (Prod.snd Nat Nat a))
    h

⑱.2 第二个因子：p ~ p' ⇒ q * p ~ q * p'

练习 8mulRep_wd_snd定理附录解答

把 q * p 用交换律换成 p * q；对 p' 同理；中间那一步正是单元⑪ 的 mulRep_wd。

theorem mulRep_wd_snd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :=
  sorry
提示 1思路

先用 mulRep_comm 把两边都换成「p 在前、q 在后」，化成 mulRep_wd；再用两次 Eq.subst 沿交换律的等式把结论搬回「q 在前」。

提示 2目标形态
intRelpp'⇒intRel(mulRepqp)(mulRepqp')
提示 3关键件

mulRep_comm（两次，注意方向：q*p = p*q 与 p'*q = q*p'）· mulRep_wd（固定第一个因子）· Eq.subst（一次在 intRel 的第一个位置、一次在第二个位置）。

查看解答 定理 · 20 行
theorem mulRep_wd_snd (a b a' b' c d : Nat)
    (h : intRel (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :
    intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b')) :=
  @Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
    (fun (y : Prod Nat Nat) =>
      intRel (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b)) y)
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
    (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b'))
    (mulRep_comm a' b' c d)
    (@Eq.subst.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
      (fun (x : Prod Nat Nat) =>
        intRel x (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a' b') (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d)))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
      (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
      (@Eq.symm.{1} (Prod Nat Nat)
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d) (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b))
        (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat a b) (@prod_mk Nat Nat c d))
        (mulRep_comm c d a b))
      (mulRep_wd a b a' b' c d h))

演示：具体数字上走一遍 —— (2-0) ~ (3-1)，都用 (4-1) 乘在前面，结果仍等价。(4,1)*(2,0) = (8,2)，(4,1)*(3,1) = (13,7)，而 8+7 = 13+2 = 15。

           (mulRep (@prod_mk Nat Nat 4 1) (@prod_mk Nat Nat 3 1)) :=

  1.   @Eq.refl.{1} Nat 15
